# Book NOTATION (one symbol, one meaning; first-use section). Standalone: the book's own table, built from the campaign conventions but containing no internal labels. The editor merges rows proposed in SEAMS files.

Planned symbols of Chap. 8 and Parts II–III were added on 2026-11-01 from SPINE-II (rows from 𝒪_i on; clash entries 50–69), and
revised on 2026-11-02 after the Parts II–III reviews (SPINE-II-REV; clash entry 70 lists the renames and the added rows). Planned Part I symbols (seeded 2026-11-01 from the Part I spine; revised the same day after the spine reviews: first-use sections follow
the revised Chaps. 4 and 6, and the renames and new rows are listed in the SPINE change log, second entry). Natural units ħ = c = k_B = 1 and
Heaviside–Lorentz charge (e² = 4πα) throughout (App. A); the Units column gives the natural-unit dimension and, where useful, the
conventional unit in which values are quoted. A decoration (subscript, superscript, alphabet, bold) is part of a symbol's name and may not
be dropped. The rows proposed for the accepted Chapters 1–2 and Appendix A were merged on 2026-11-02 (clash entry 71 lists the renames,
the flags and the moved first uses; ledgers/check_notation.py checks the tex against this table). The rows proposed for the accepted Appendix E (E.1–E.13) were merged on 2026-11-02 (clash entry 72 lists the renames, the spelling splits and the flags; the App. E rows close the table). The rows proposed for the accepted Chapter 4 and Appendix D.6–D.12 were merged on 2026-11-02 after the Chapter 4 gate (clash entry 73; their new rows follow the planned Sect. 4.9 rows). The rows proposed for the accepted Chapter 3, App. C.1–C.8 and App. D.4–D.5, and for the drafted App. C.10–C.20, were merged on 2026-11-02 (clash entry 74; the Chap. 3 and D.4–D.5 rows follow the planned Sect. 3.6 rows, the App. C rows precede the App. E rows). The rows proposed for the accepted Chapter 5, App. D.13–D.15 and App. C.18 were merged on 2026-11-02 (clash entry 75; the new rows follow the planned Sect. 5.7 rows). The rows proposed for the accepted Chapter 6 and App. D.16–D.18 were merged on 2026-11-02 (clash entry 76; the new rows follow the planned Sect. 6.5 rows). The rows proposed for the accepted Chapter 7 and App. D.19–D.23 were merged on 2026-11-02 (clash entry 77; the new rows follow the planned rows of Sects. 7.1–7.4). The rows proposed for the accepted Chapter 8 and App. D.24–D.28 were merged on 2026-11-02 (clash entry 78; the new rows follow the planned Chap. 8 rows, after ϑ_k; ρ_c(q) is dropped; the pair propagator of D.12 is now 𝔏). The rows proposed for the accepted Chapter 10 and App. D.29–D.30 were merged on 2026-11-02 (clash entry 79; the new rows follow the planned Chap. 10 rows, after v_χ,max; 𝒢_GS is 𝒢_rel, and the Euler gamma function of 10.5 is upright Γ). The rows proposed for the accepted Chapter 11 and App. D.31–D.32 were merged on 2026-11-02 (clash entry 80: the 33 rows of the Ch11 revision follow the Γ_iso row, the rows added at the merge follow the M_sph row). The rows proposed for the accepted Chapter 14 and App. D.38 were merged on 2026-11-06 (clash entry 85; the Ch14 rows of the revision and of the merge follow the S^PA_𝒯𝒯 row, before the planned Chap. 15 rows). The rows of the accepted Chapter 9 were merged on 2026-11-07 (clash entry 95: the six rows of the Ch9 revision follow the m_min^ac row and are kept; the merge adds M_Ge after the ○ row and moves the first use of c_M, c_LA and M_Si to 9.2). Clashes are flagged at the end; ⚑ in a row points to that list, and every ⚑ row has an entry there.
Three-volume edition (2026-11-12): symbols no longer used in the tex have first use "— (removed in the three-volume edition)"; where only some symbols of a row went, the row is split and the removed ones follow it in a row of their own; first uses in the folded App. D.15, D.17, D.18, D.19, D.29, D.41 (now in Sects. 5.6, 6.4, 6.5, 7.1, 10.1, 16.1) and in sections whose use was cut are retargeted; D.14 now holds only the Fröhlich vertex. Log: ledgers/REVIEWS/volumes/notation.md.

| Symbol | Meaning | Units | First use |
|---|---|---|---|
| ħ, c, k_B | set to 1 | — | 1.1 |
| α | fine-structure constant ⚑ | 1 | 1.3 |
| e | elementary charge, e > 0, e² = 4πα; the electron charge is −e ⚑ | 1 | 1.4 |
| m_e | electron mass | eV | 1.3 |
| G_N | Newton's constant | eV⁻² | — (1.1 use removed 2026-11-02; unused) |
| M_enc(r) | mass enclosed within galactocentric radius r ⚑ | M_⊙ | — (1.1 use removed 2026-11-02; unused) |
| v_c(r) | circular velocity of a galactic orbit ⚑ | km s⁻¹ | 1.1 (qualitative; Jeans argument in 1.2) |
| ρ_crit, h | critical density of the Universe, 3H_Hub,0²/(8πG_N); dimensionless Hubble parameter, the Hubble rate today in units of 100 km s⁻¹ Mpc⁻¹ ⚑ | eV⁴; 1 | ρ_crit: — (1.1 use removed 2026-11-02; critical density named in words); h: 1.1 (in Ω_c h² only) |
| Ω_c h² | cosmological dark-matter density parameter (Ω_c only in this combination) ⚑ | 1 | 1.1 |
| H_Hub | Hubble rate (subscript mandatory) ⚑ | eV | — (1.1 use removed 2026-11-02; unused) |
| M_Pl | Planck mass | eV | — (1.1 use removed 2026-11-02; "Planck mass" in words only) |
| ⟨σv⟩ | thermally averaged annihilation cross-section times relative speed ⚑ | cm³ s⁻¹ | 1.1 |
| χ | the dark-matter particle (only) ⚑ | — | 1.1 |
| m_χ | dark-matter mass | eV | 1.1 |
| H_Hub,0 | Hubble rate today, 100h km s⁻¹ Mpc⁻¹ (never H₀, which is the unperturbed Hamiltonian of 2.1) ⚑ | eV | — (1.1 use removed 2026-11-02; unused) |
| ρ_halo(r) | dark-matter halo mass density at galactocentric radius r (singular isothermal sphere, ρ_halo ∝ r⁻²) ⚑ | eV⁴; GeV cm⁻³ | 1.2 (moved from 1.1, 2026-11-02) |
| r_⊙ | galactocentric radius of the Sun, 8 kpc | kpc | — (1.1 use removed 2026-11-02; unused) |
| ρ_rad | radiation energy density of the early Universe ⚑ | eV⁴ | — (1.1 use removed 2026-11-02; unused) |
| g_* | effective number of relativistic degrees of freedom (entropy and energy counts taken equal; ≈ 80 at 100 GeV, 10.75 at 1 GeV) ⚑ | 1 | — (1.1 use removed 2026-11-02; unused) |
| T_fo, x_fo | freeze-out temperature; x_fo = m_χ/T_fo | eV; 1 | — (1.1 use removed 2026-11-02; unused) |
| s, s_0 | entropy density of the Universe; its present value 2.22 × 10⁻³⁸ GeV³ (bare s as a quantity only here; spin labels s, s′ only as labels and subscripts, 2.3) ⚑ | eV³ | — (1.1 use removed 2026-11-02; unused) |
| 𝓃_χ | dark-matter number density ρ_χ/m_χ (script n, macro \nd) | eV³; cm⁻³ | 1.1 |
| g_deg | degeneracy factor (number of internal states): spin degeneracy 2 of the electron–hole bubble and of the singlet exciton (4.6, 4.9; App. D.12, E.6); spin × cone (7.3); of χ in the equilibrium density (1.1, removed 2026-11-02) ⚑ | 1 | 4.6 (moved from 7.3 at the Chap. 4 merge; also App. E.4, E.6) |
| ρ_χ | local dark-matter mass density, 0.3 GeV cm⁻³ | eV⁴; GeV cm⁻³ | 1.1 (eq:1.1-rate; value fixed in 1.2) |
| f_χ(v) | dark-matter velocity distribution in the laboratory frame ⚑ | (velocity)⁻³ | 1.1 (eq:1.1-rate; model in 1.2) |
| v | dark-matter velocity in the laboratory frame (bare v only) ⚑ | 1; km s⁻¹ | 1.1 |
| v₀ | circular speed parameter of the standard halo model, 238 km/s | km s⁻¹ | 1.2 |
| v_esc | galactic escape speed, 544 km/s | km s⁻¹ | 1.2 |
| v_⊙ | solar velocity in the galactic frame, \|v_⊙\| = 250.55 km/s (Baxter et al., Table 1) | km s⁻¹ | 1.2 |
| v_⊕(t) | Earth's orbital velocity relative to the Sun | km s⁻¹ | 1.2 |
| v_E(t) | Earth velocity in the galactic frame, v_⊙ + v_⊕(t) | km s⁻¹ | 1.2 |
| v_E^ref | reference Earth speed for thresholds and edges: the adopted value of \|v_⊙\|, 250.5 km/s | km s⁻¹ | 1.2 |
| v_max | maximum laboratory speed v_esc + v_E^ref = 794.5 km/s | km s⁻¹ | 1.2 |
| N_esc | normalization of the truncated halo distribution ⚑ | (velocity)³ | 1.2 |
| Φ_χ | dark-matter number flux (subscript mandatory) ⚑ | cm⁻² s⁻¹ | 1.2 |

| F_χ(v) | laboratory speed distribution, ∫dv F_χ = 1 ⚑ | (velocity)⁻¹ | 1.2 |
| ω_yr, ω_sid | orbital angular frequency of the Earth, 0.0172 d⁻¹; sidereal angular frequency 2π/(23 h 56 min 4 s) ⚑ | d⁻¹; s⁻¹ | 1.2 |
| t_peak, t_E | date of largest Earth speed; time at which a laboratory axis and v_E share a meridian | d; h | 1.2 |

| ℓ_lat | geographic latitude (subscript mandatory; ℓ_Qν is the zero-point length) ⚑ | rad | 1.2 |
| u_esc | local escape speed of an attracting body (Sun, Earth), focusing estimate only ⚑ | km s⁻¹ | 1.2 |

| m_min | threshold mass 2E_th/v_max² | eV | 1.3 |
| q₀ | reference momentum αm_e | eV | 1.3 |

| θ | angle between q and v (1.3); an integration angle elsewhere, defined where it is used (the scattering angle is θ_s) | rad | 1.3 |

| E_R | nuclear recoil energy | eV | 1.3 |
| E_g | indirect (fundamental) band gap; qualitative in 1.3, precise in 3.3. Chapter 1 uses the literature value 1.11 eV (Essig et al., Eq. (5.2)) for every threshold mass; measured 1.12 eV at 300 K and E_g(0) = 1.1692 eV (Ramanathan and Kurinsky) | eV | 1.3 |

| Ṽ(q) | Fourier transform of the dark-matter–electron potential, ∫d³x e^{+iq·x}V(x); in the effective theory of 8.1 the full non-relativistic potential Σ_i c_i^{(e)}𝒪_i, an operator in both spin spaces, which reduces to the Yukawa Ṽ of 1.4 when only c₁^{(e)} ≠ 0 ⚑ | eV⁻² | 1.1 (eq:1.1-rate; derived in 1.4; widened in 8.1) |

| \|n⟩ | generic unperturbed eigenstate (label inside kets only; not the band index n or the occupation n_Qν) ⚑ | — | 2.1 |

| dΩ_q | solid-angle element of the scattered momentum (subscript mandatory) ⚑ | sr | 2.2 |
| f_{i→f}(q) | single-particle transition form factor ⟨f\|e^{iq·r}\|i⟩ ⚑ | 1 | 2.2 |
| F_1s(q) | hydrogen 1s elastic form factor 16/(4 + q²a₀²)² | 1 | 2.2 |
| a₀ | Bohr radius | Å | 2.2 |
| F_Helm(q) | Helm nuclear form factor | 1 | 2.2 |
| θ_s | scattering angle between initial and final relative momenta (the center-of-mass angle of 19.3) (subscript mandatory) | rad | 2.2 (also 19.3) |
| p, p′; 𝐏_tot, 𝐩_rel | relative momenta before and after a two-body collision (2.2 Born derivation); total and relative momenta of a two-body system (subscripts mandatory; bare bold 𝐏 is the polarization) ⚑ | eV | 2.2 |

## Clashes flagged (2026-11-01; revised after the spine reviews)
Each letter below carries, or could carry, two meanings in the material the writers read. The book's choice is in the table; writers must
not use the alternatives. **Confirmed by the editor (2026-11-01): entries 1–26 stand as written, with the revisions marked "revised",
which apply the editor's decision on the spine reviews (SPINE change log, second entry); the adopted rows φ_ext, z_qp, W^scr, g_ph and
ρ_c were adopted; ρ_c was dropped at the Chap. 8 merge, entry 78, unused).** Entries 27 onward are new in the revision.
1. **χ.** The dark-matter particle only. Response functions are R (many papers and textbooks write χ). App. B.2 gives the correspondence.
   Kinetic mixing, written χ in part of the dark-photon literature, is ζ (entry 12).
2. **ε.** Dielectric function ε(q, ω) (and ε₁, ε₂, ε^T, ε_GG′, ε_mac, ε_∞, ε₀, ε_bg, ε_Lin, ε_Mer, ε₂^PA); band energy ε_nk (always two
   subscripts) and ε^ren_nk; ε_eh (energy per pair). Kinetic mixing, commonly ε in the literature, is ζ. The vacuum permittivity appears
   only when quoting an SI source formula, as ε₀^SI; bare ε₀ is the static dielectric constant.
3. **v (revised).** Bare v is the dark-matter velocity; v as a band label only as a subscript; v_F the Fermi velocity of the free
   electron gas (2.5, 4.5) only; the Dirac velocity is v_D (7.3; the literature writes v_F); v_c(r) the galactic circular velocity
   (1.1 only; subscript c is upright text, not the band label); v^g_n group velocity and v_mn velocity matrix elements (two band
   indices; v_nn = v^g_n); v_N nuclear velocity; v^B_k BCS factor and v^M_k magnon coefficient (superscripts mandatory); v_C(k) the
   Coulomb kernel (upright C); v_xc the exchange–correlation potential.
4. **c.** c = 1 (speed of light); c, c† fermion operators; c as a band label only as a subscript; c_ν sound speed. Writers avoid c_s for
   sound speed.
5. **a.** Lattice constant a; 𝐚_i primitive vectors (bold); a₀ Bohr radius; a* effective Bohr radius; a_x exciton radius; a_kν magnon
   operators (always with subscripts). Holstein–Primakoff textbooks also use a for bosons; the book keeps a_kν only for magnons and b_Qν
   only for phonons.
6. **b.** 𝐛_i reciprocal primitive vectors (bold, index i); b_Qν phonon operators (always Q and ν). Never write a bare b.
7. **n.** n, m band indices (subscripts only); n_e(q) density operator; n_B, n_F distribution functions; n_Qν phonon occupation; number
   densities use script 𝓃 (𝓃(r), 𝓃_e, 𝓃_D). The common DFT symbol n(r) is not used.
8. **G (revised).** Reciprocal-lattice vector. The Green's function is sans-serif 𝖦 (macro \GF); G_N is Newton's constant (1.1 only); G_LF
   the local-field factor; the Van Hove function is script 𝒢_VH(r, t) (papers write G(r, t)). Writers check that the font distinctions
   survive in the LaTeX macros.
9. **D (revised).** D^dyn dynamical matrix (superscript mandatory); D^n deformation-potential tensor (band superscript); 𝒟_f(E) the density
   of final states (script); D.n are appendix labels in prose only. The phonon density of states is g_ph(ω) (5.2).
10. **Γ, X, L, K.** Γ (italic) rates; Γ_w a width; upright Γ, X, L, K the high-symmetry points of the zone. Italic L is the loss function
    (4.4); italic K is not used in Part I (K̄ is the mean kinetic energy, entry 41). The writer of 3.1 states the typographic rule
    (upright for zone points).
11. **W.** W_κ(q) Debye–Waller exponent only; the screened interaction is W^scr (first use 4.8, superscript mandatory). Many papers write
    the loss function as W; the book writes L.
12. **κ (revised).** κ is the species index only (bare κ, as a subscript or a summation index). The Born–Oppenheimer small parameter is
    κ_BO, a scalar that always carries its subscript and is never summed or used as an index. Kinetic mixing, formerly κ_mix, is renamed
    ζ, so no Part II formula combines a mixing parameter with a sum over species κ. Background dielectric constants are ε_bg, not κ.
13. **Z.** Z (atomic number, 1.4) and Z_κ nuclear charge; Z*_κ Born charges; 𝒵 partition function. The quasiparticle residue is z_qp
    (4.7).
14. **Δ.** Δ_χ mass splitting, Δ_SO, Δ_scis, Δ_BCS, Δ_D: the subscript is mandatory; bare Δ is reserved for differences.
15. **S (revised).** S(q, ω), S_ab: response objects; S^st(q) the static structure factor; script 𝒮_O(ω) the transition sum of 2.5
    (identified with S_ab in 4.2); S_HR Huang–Rhys; 𝐒_j spins (bold). Spin operators of the dark matter, if needed, are 𝐒_χ.
16. **Σ.** Self-energy Σ versus summation sign: the self-energy always carries arguments or a superscript.
17. **α (revised).** Fine-structure constant α; α_F Fröhlich constant; α_abs absorption coefficient (the optics literature writes α(ω));
    α̃ Dirac coupling; α²F(ω) the Eliashberg function, read as one symbol (its α is not the fine-structure constant; never factor it);
    Cartesian indices use α, β only as superscripts on vectors and tensors and are introduced as such.
18. **λ (revised).** Exciton label λ; the electron–phonon coupling constant is λ_ep (subscript mandatory). Wavelengths are not needed in
    Part I.
19. **ρ.** ρ_χ, ρ_T mass densities; ρ_crit the critical density; ρ_mn(k, q) density vertex; ϱ̂ density matrix (varrho, hat). The charge
    density is e n_Z (revised at the Chap. 8 merge: the planned ρ_c(q) is dropped, entry 78).
20. **y, g.** Mediator couplings y_χ, y_e, y_N (the literature often writes g_χ, g_e); g_mnν electron–phonon vertex; g_aee, g_aγγ axion
    couplings; g_deg degeneracy; g_ph(ω) phonon density of states. The electron g-factor, if needed, is g_e only in magnetic contexts
    (Chap. 7 does not need it).
21. **φ (revised).** Absorbed bosonic dark-matter field φ (and m_φ, 𝐩_φ); the external potential of 4.1 is φ_ext(t); PAW partial waves are
    φ_j^AE, φ_j^PS (superscripts mandatory, 3.5 only).
22. **J.** J^ex_ij exchange (superscript mandatory; J^LKAG_ij is the source-convention LKAG constant of E.9, entry 72); j_e(q) current (lower case).
23. **Q.** Phonon wavevector Q; sources that write the phonon wavevector as q are converted (App. B.2), since q is the dark-matter momentum
    transfer. The ionization yield, written Q in some papers, is N_eh.
24. **M (revised).** Generic nuclear mass M in Chaps. 1–2 and in κ_BO; M_κ per species from 5.2; M_enc(r) the galactic enclosed mass
    (1.1; was M(r)); M_Pl the Planck mass; 𝓜¹ moments (script).
25. **R.** Bravais lattice vector R (bold italic, as every vector, STYLE) and the response function R (not bold; R_ab, R̃, R⁰). The
    vector rule separates them; writers never use a non-bold R for a lattice vector. ℛ (script) is the event rate.
26. **A.** Mass number A (A_κ per species); exciton envelope A^λ_{cvk} (indices mandatory); spectral function sans-serif 𝖠(k, ω).
27. **ω (new).** Bare ω is the energy given to the target. Every other frequency carries a mandatory decoration: ω_q (kinematic transfer),
    ω_p (plasma frequency, a constant), ω_pl(q) (plasmon dispersion), ω_Qν (phonon), ω_L, ω_T (polar optical modes), ω_D (Debye),
    ω^F_j, ω^B_j (Matsubara). Writers never write ω_q for a phonon, never drop the ν from ω_Qν, and never write ω_ph except in an estimate
    stated in words ("a typical phonon energy").
28. **V (new).** Bare V is the normalization volume only (superseded by entry 83: the volume is script 𝓥, `\Vol`). Potentials always carry an argument or a decoration: V(x) and Ṽ(q) (dark matter),
    V_KS (Kohn–Sham), v_C(k), v_xc (lower case), and ∂_{κ,Q}V_KS. The mediator is V only inside the subscript of m_V. Interaction
    Hamiltonians are H′, H_int, H_ep.
29. **Ω (new).** Bare Ω is the unit-cell volume. The solid angle is dΩ_q (2.2; the literature writes dΩ); the Wannier spread is Ω_W
    (3.6); the cosmological density appears only as Ω_c h². No other Ω is used in Part I.
30. **Φ (new).** Φ^{(n)} force constants (superscript mandatory, κκ′ indices as subscripts); the displacement correlation, formerly
    Φ_κκ′(t), is renamed 𝒞_κκ′(t) (5.6), so the two κκ′ matrices of Chap. 5 have different letters; Φ_χ (subscript mandatory) is the
    dark-matter number flux of 1.2.
31. **μ (new).** μ_χe, μ_χN, μ_χp, μ_x are reduced masses (subscripts mandatory); the chemical potential is μ_ch (2.5), which enters n_F at
    T > 0 and becomes E_F at T = 0. Bare μ is not used (the Born cross-section of 2.2 writes the reduced mass with its subscript).
32. **σ (new).** Cross-sections carry a bar or a particle subscript (σ̄_e, σ_p, σ_N, ⟨σv⟩); the optical conductivity is σ_cond(ω) with
    parts σ₁, σ₂ (numeric subscripts only for the conductivity); Pauli matrices σ_i appear only inside s_e(q) with a particle index.
33. **γ (new).** γ_rel relaxation rate (4.5); γ_Qν phonon linewidth with parts γ^anh_Qν (5.7) and γ^ep_Qν (6.4).
34. **f (new).** f_χ(v) velocity distribution; f_{i→f}(q) transition form factor; f_nk occupation; f^osc_n0 oscillator strength; f_xc,
    f_Hxc kernels; "f-sum" is a name, not a symbol. Each always carries its decoration.
35. **u (new).** u_nk periodic Bloch part (band and k subscripts); u_Rκ displacement (lattice and species subscripts); u^B_k, u^M_k
    coefficients (superscripts mandatory).
36. **B (new).** B_κ displacement tensor (one species index, a tensor in Cartesian indices) and B_Qν displacement operator (phonon labels):
    the indices separate them; writers never write a bare B.
37. **e (new).** e the elementary charge; e_κν(Q) phonon polarization vectors (always both indices and the argument); upright e the
    exponential.
38. **N (new).** Bare N is the number of cells; N_esc, N_eh carry mandatory subscripts; the Fermi-level density of states is script 𝒩_F
    (2.5; formerly N(0) in 7.2).
39. **η (new).** η(v_min) the mean inverse speed (always with its argument); η_anh the anharmonic parameter.
40. **U (new).** U_G potential Fourier components; U(k) the gauge matrix (argument k); U_H the on-site repulsion (7.1).
41. **T, K (new).** T temperature; T_c critical temperature; T_fi T-matrix element (two subscripts); the mean kinetic energy, formerly ⟨T⟩
    in 4.3, is K̄.
42. **k, p (new).** k crystal momentum; the momentum of an absorbed boson, formerly |k| ≈ m_φv in 1.4, is 𝐩_φ; p_mn momentum matrix
    elements (two band indices); p_i Gibbs weights (one state index); p̃_j PAW projectors (tilde mandatory).
43. **Θ, τ (new).** Θ(x) Heaviside function (always with its argument) and Θ_D Debye temperature; τ imaginary time (4.7). Lifetimes are
    written 1/Γ or through Im Σ, not τ.
44. **Π (new).** Π_ν(Q, ω) the phonon self-energy (6.4); products over indices are written in words or with ∏ in displayed formulas that
    contain no Π_ν.
45. **H, h (new).** H′, H_int, H_ep, H^W(R) Hamiltonians (each decorated); H_Hub the Hubble rate; h the dimensionless Hubble parameter
    (only in Ω_c h² and ρ_crit); ħ = 1.
46. **t (new).** t time; t_hop the hopping amplitude (subscript mandatory).
47. **ζ (new).** Kinetic mixing (1.4) only; App. B.2 lists the literature's ε, κ and χ for it.
48. **F (new).** F_DM, F_Helm, F_1s, F^mag form factors (decorated); F_κ the Hellmann–Feynman force (species index); α²F the Eliashberg
    function (entry 17).
49. **ℰ (new).** The macroscopic electric field is script ℰ (5.5), because E is reserved for energies (E_i, E_g, E_k, …).

**Clashes flagged for Chap. 8 and Parts II–III (SPINE-II, 2026-11-01).** The research files use several letters that the book reserves;
the book names below replace them (writer side: the research symbol is given in parentheses and never printed).
50. **Truncation label.** 𝔗 (research: τ). Bare τ stays imaginary time (entry 43). The components n_B, n_g, n_A, n_T, n_D are orders, always italic (no upright form; entry 72);
    n_B with an argument, n_B(x), stays the Bose function: writers always write the order as "n_B = 1" style integers and the function with
    its argument.
51. **Sectors.** 𝒫_ad, 𝒫_0, 𝒫_+ and the words "electron-following", "zero-phonon electronic", "phonon-dressed electronic" (research:
    GS, X0, X+, which are banned terms). "Dyson convention" replaces the research label of the convention.
52. **η.** Entry 39 keeps η for η(v_min) and η_anh. The research's η_ep is θ_ep; its η^φ_NR is Y_ph; its η_pb is Y_qp; its κ_η keeps κ
    only with its subscript (entry 12: κ bare is the species index).
53. **ζ.** Kinetic mixing only (entry 47). The research's collection contrasts ζ_O, ζ_gap are ϖ_O, ϖ_gap.
54. **κ.** The research's κ_cal is C_cal and its κ_URF is not used (stated in words). κ_η (11.1) and κ_BO are the only decorated κ.
55. **r, y, g.** The research's r_coll is 𝔯_coll (fraktur; r_s, r_c, r_IP are distinct decorated letters); y_ion is f_ion (y_χ, y_e, y_N stay
    couplings; bare y in 15.2 is the edge variable ω/Δ_BCS, local to Chap. 15); g_Luke is the Luke gain (renamed from g_L, entry 91) (g_mnν, g_ph, g_deg, g^AH are distinct).
56. **Λ.** Bare Λ is the final-state class (8.7); Λ_D (7.3) keeps its subscript.
57. **Q, Ω, f, τ in transport.** The phonon wavevector keeps Q (entry 23): the research's Q_prompt is E_prompt and the boosted-flux Q is 𝒬.
    The phonon energy in Chap. 20 is E_ath (renamed from E_ph in entry 71; research: f and Ω, which clash with the f family and the cell volume Ω). The film escape time is
    t_l and the pair-breaking time t₀ (research: τ_l, τ₀; τ is imaginary time).
58. **𝒞 and 𝒱.** 𝒞_κκ′(t) stays the displacement correlation (entry 30); the covariance term of F_eh is 𝒱_<> (research: 𝒞_<>).
59. **F.** F_eh, F_meas, F_NR join the decorated F family of entry 48; bare F is not used.
60. **ϵ.** The research's ϵ_det (lunate) is f_det, so that ε keeps its dielectric meanings only.
61. **K.** The research's coupling K of a tunnelling defect is K_b; K stays a high-symmetry point (entry 41) and K̄ the mean kinetic energy.
62. **P.** P_Λ, P_T, P_Mig, P_casc, P(first) and P̄ are decorated; bold 𝐏 stays the polarization (5.5). p_surv is lower case.
63. **ϱ.** ϱ̂ (hat) is the density matrix; ϱ_MK the Markov–Krein band.
64. **D.** 𝔇_ab (fraktur) is the dark-matter-side tensor; 𝒟_f(E) (script) the density of final states; D^dyn, D^n keep their decorations.
65. **Y.** Y_q, Y_ph, Y_qp are three yields, each with its subscript; bare Y is not used.
66. **ω.** Entry 27 extended: ω_l, ω_e (joint response), ω_th (threshold), ω_p^val, ω_AFMR, ω_E, ω_A carry mandatory decorations.
67. **𝒪 and O.** 𝒪_i are the effective operators (script); O_a(q) (italic) stay the target operators of 4.1.
68. **v.** v^⊥ (transverse velocity) and v_χ,max (boosted flux) join bare v (dark-matter velocity); v_D, v_F, v_N keep their meanings.
69. **Remaining flags.** 𝒯_e (script T; T is temperature, T_fi the T-matrix); ϑ_k (nuisance; θ is an angle, Θ the Heaviside function);
    f_crys joins the f family of entry 34; ν̃ (tilde mandatory; ν is the phonon branch); W_AB (two material labels; W_κ(q) is the
    Debye–Waller exponent, W^scr the screened interaction, W_∥/W_⊥ the spin weights); μ_red joins entry 31; ΔS (spin reduction) and
    λ_S (spin–lattice factor; λ alone is the exciton label, λ_ep the coupling); Z*(ω) (with argument) against static Z*_κ; C^IR, A^IR,
    B^IR (superscripts mandatory; B_κ, B_Qν keep entry 36, A^λ entry 26); θ_ii (subscript mandatory); E_st (stored energy; E_st never
    abbreviates anything else).
70. **Clashes and missing symbols of the Parts II–III review (SPINE-II-REV, 2026-11-02; structural review §8).** Renames adopted:
    Σ₁, Σ₂ (20.3) → 𝔫₁, 𝔫₂ (Σ stays the self-energy); the bare spin length S (14.1) → \|𝐒\| (S stays the structure factor); bare v in
    t_l (20.3) → v_s; bare c in m_min^ac (9.2) → c_ν; bare F(q) (12.5) → F_cr(q) (entry 59); δ₀ (13.6) → δ_w (tanδ₀ of 16.7 keeps
    δ₀); ℓ_mfp (15.3) → 𝔩_e (ℓ stays the cubic index, ℓ_Qν the phonon length); m_a (10.8) → m_φ; μ_eχ (16.3) → μ_χe; 𝓃_e(R_κ) (8.3) →
    𝓃_e^{(κ)}; n(ω) (12.6) → n_r(ω) (n stays a band index). Landé g is always g_J (the bare g is the vertex). η(c + u) (17.5) is not a
    new symbol: it is η(v_min) of 1.3 at v_min = c + u, written in full (entry 52). Rows added: c_LA, B, q_BZ, x (10.8), F_cr, 𝓃_e^{(κ)},
    n_r, g_J, \|𝐒\|, T_CM, T₁, T₂, 𝔤_IL^nor, 𝔩_e, a_s, N_ch, v_a, ε_in, ε_out, E_r, q̃, a_mol, 𝓃_c, ε_r, σ_χN, T_hi, T_lo, c_NR1, ρ∞, k₂,
    r, ω₀, Ī, 𝔫₁, 𝔫₂, v_s, P_abs, λ_d; first uses moved with their sections (𝐁₀ 10.7, S_ℓ 11.7, W_AB 17.5, M_cr 19.6, ν̃ 8.1).
71. **Merge of the accepted Chapters 1–2 and Appendix A (SEAMS 1.1–1.5, 2.1–2.5, A, with their revision rows; 2026-11-02).**
    (a) Renames of symbols the accepted text still prints, applied at the next touch of the file (the row gives the new name; the old
    one is used nowhere else): bare μ (1.3, reduced mass with a free target) → μ_χT (entry 31); bare Π (1.4, photon self-energy) → Π_em
    (entry 44); bare b (1.5, background rate) → ℛ_bkg (entry 6); g(ξ), g(p), δE_g (2.1 test functions) → w(ξ), w(p), Δ_w as in 2.4
    (entry 20; the bare g is the vertex); bare bold 𝐏 (2.2, total momentum) → 𝐏_tot (entry 62: bold 𝐏 is the polarization); Ô₁, Ô₂
    (2.4, target operators of the two couplings) → Ô_a, Ô_b (Ô_1, Ô_2 are the one- and two-body operators of 2.3; O_a as in 4.1); bare
    Δ (2.5, two-level splitting) → Δ_tl (entry 14); bare λ (2.5, oscillator coupling) → λ_osc (entry 18); G(x) (C.9, Green's function)
    → G_Y(x) (entry 8; G is the reciprocal-lattice vector from 1.3); K(τ) (D.3, memory kernel) → K(t − t′) (entry 43: τ is imaginary time).
    (b) Renames the revisers already made in the text (the rows follow the text): b, β of the hydrogen integrals → λ_1s, λ_2p (2.2); the
    target states a, b → i_T, f_T (2.4); δ_w → Δ_w (2.4; δ_w stays the off-shell margin of 13.6); ξ(v_min) → η_2(v_min) (A.8); the pole
    residue Z → r_p (D.3); χ_nl → ψ̃_nl (2.2); bare σ, μ of A.9 → σ_T, σ_N, μ_χe, μ_χN; the charge numbers z_e, z_κ are withdrawn (2.3, A.2).
    (c) Planned rows renamed so that the accepted text keeps its symbol: the athermal phonon energy E_ph (20.2, 20.4) → E_ath (E_ph is the
    Luke-amplified phonon energy of 1.5); the bias voltage V_b (19.5) → V_bias (printed in 1.5); r, ω₀ of the Klein relation (18.4) →
    r_K, ω_K (ω₀ is the oscillator frequency of 2.5).
    (d) Flags without a rename. H_Hub,0 (Hubble rate today) against H₀ (unperturbed Hamiltonian, 2.1); entry 45 extended: bare H is the
    full Hamiltonian only, and H₀, H₁, H₂, H_ee, H_jel, H_C, H_Coul, H_s, H_v are decorated. Bare s is the entropy density (1.1); spin
    projections s, s′ (2.3) are labels and subscripts only; s_skin, s_e(q) are decorated. ℳ (invariant amplitude, 2.1; ℳ_free in A.9)
    never carries a numeric superscript, which marks the moment matrices 𝓜^k. g_deg is widened to one meaning, a degeneracy factor (the
    internal states of χ in 1.1, spin × cone in 7.3); g_*, g_φee, y_p, y_n join entry 20; ρ_halo, ρ_rad, ρ_s join entry 19; ω_yr,
    ω_sid, ω₀, ω₁, ω₂ join entry 27; the right ascensions α_E, α_z, α_n̂ join entry 17; δ_E (declination) and δ₁, δ₂ (detunings) are
    decorated (bare δ is the delta function); h_E (hour angle) joins entry 45; ℓ_lat against ℓ_Qν; u_esc joins entry 35; ϑ, ϑ_cut
    (local to 1.2) against ϑ_k (8.8); θ (1.3) against the decorated θ_s, θ_E, θ_lab, θ_n̂; F_χ(v), F_N(q), F_Fermi join entry 48; ξ
    (2.1, 2.4) against ξ_C (2.2) and ξ_k (7.2); Γ_i joins entry 10; c_n(t) (time argument mandatory) against c, c† (entry 4); T̂ (hat)
    against T, T_fi (entry 41); \|n⟩, n_γ and the atomic n, l, m against the band index n (entry 7); 𝛔 (bold, 1.4) against σ_i inside
    s_e(q), and σ_T, σ̄_e^lim (entry 32); J^μ currents against J^ex (entry 22); 𝐄, 𝐁, 𝐀 (bold) against energies E, ℰ, B_κ (entries 36,
    49); ℒ (calligraphic) against the loss function L; ψ, ψ̄, γ^μ, γ⁵ (1.4) against ψ_nk, γ_rel, γ_Qν and the state labels γ (2.3);
    φ_V, φ₀ join entry 21; R_nuc, 𝐑_cm, R(z) (D.3) against R (entry 25); c_nuc, a_nuc against c, a (entries 4, 5); X_I, I and the upright
    I of \|ψ_I⟩; U_1, U_2, U(x) (C.9), U(t) (2.5), 𝒰 (D.2), U_I join entry 40; d (spacing, 2.3 only) against d_γ (bosons); E_γ, E_m,
    E_B, E_ej, E_p, E_ph, E_prim, E_w join entry 49; N_sig, N_obs, N_bkg, N_90, N_f, N_e join entry 38; ℛ_n (n a count), ℛ_bkg, ℛ_toy
    against ℛ; M_det joins entry 24; A_mod, A_μ against A (entry 26); Δ_tl, ΔE_i, Δ_w join entry 14; r(m_χ) (argument mandatory) against
    the radius r and r_p; G_111 (Miller index) and G_Y join entry 8; μ_χT joins entry 31; P_{i→f}(t) joins entry 62. Also flagged:
    χ₁, χ₂, m_χ₁, m_χ₂ (inelastic states; bare χ stays the dark matter, entry 1); τ_s (switching time) against τ (entry 43); Ô, O_fi
    (2.1) against O_a (4.1) and 𝒪_i (entry 67); e_G, e_SI, ε₀^SI (quoted Gaussian and SI conventions; e is Heaviside–Lorentz, entries 2,
    37); \|f_ion\|² joins entry 34; n_Z(k) against ρ_c (entry 19; ρ_c dropped in entry 78); 𝐩_rel, 𝐫′_j, m_T against p, r, the band index m; \|m⟩ (an intermediate
    state, inside kets) against the band index m; s_0 with s; z_p (D.3 pole) against z_qp.
    (e) First uses moved: v 1.2 → 1.1; Θ(x) 1.3 → 1.2; c_ν 5.2 → 1.3 (decision (i) above); G 3.1 → 1.3; ρ_T 1.5 → 1.4; 𝐁₀ 10.7 → 1.4;

    (g) Local symbols without a row (SEAM files): ℐ(v_E) (1.2); K_sp, e_cut(v), I₁–I₃ (D.1); the dummy y; s and δ of the edge expansion
    of 1.3; k (1.5, multiple of the background fluctuation); K, p (1.5, binomial derivation); c̃_f (2.1, rotating frame).
    (h) Gaps left after the merge (`python3 ledgers/check_notation.py` over tex/ch01, tex/ch02, tex/appA, 2026-11-02; no SEAM proposed a
    row, so each needs a row or a rewrite at the next touch): bare μ (1.3) and bare Π (1.4) until the renames of (a); p_e, p_χ, x_e, x_χ
    (2.1, 2.2: momenta and positions of the two particles); Ψ, Ψ_{γ₁…γ_N} (2.3, many-body wavefunction); o_γ (2.3, eigenvalue of ô);
    v̂_east, v̂_north, v̂_up (1.2, local unit vectors); 𝐄_G, 𝐁_G, 𝐄_SI, 𝐁_SI (A.2, fields in Gaussian and SI units); r_e, r_χ (A.3,
    positions); ĩ, f̃ (A.4, time-reversed states); ê_ν, b† (A.6, unit polarization; generic phonon operator); ε_e, R_ee, R̃_ee, S_ee, O_e
    (A.7, electron-channel objects); N_cell (A.8, number of cells of a target; bare N is the number of cells of the normalization volume);
    f_crystal (A.8, which should print f_crys of 10.1); q_ref, σ_ref (A.9, reference point of a conversion); bare O (generic operator,
    2.1–2.5, A.3, A.7). Bare one-letter dummies (i, j, u, z, o) were checked by grep: indices and integration variables, no row needed.
    (i) Closed 2026-11-02 (notation fix of the accepted files). The renames of (a) are applied in the text: μ_χT (1.3), Π_em (1.4),
    ℛ_bkg (1.5), w(ξ), w(p), Δ_w (2.1), P_tot, 𝐏_tot (2.2), Ô_a, Ô_b with H₁, H₂ written out (2.4), Δ_tl and λ_osc (2.5, and the labels
    of Fig. 2.6), G_Y, G̃_Y (C.9) and K(t − t′) (D.3, whose Markov step now integrates over t′ and prints no τ); Fig. 2.4 labels the
    target states i_T, f_T as in (b). The gaps of (h) have rows: v̂_east, v̂_north, v̂_up; O, O(t); 𝐩_χ, 𝐩_e, 𝐱_χ, 𝐱_e; Ψ, Ψ_{γ₁…γ_N};
    o_γ; 𝐄_G, 𝐁_G, 𝐄_SI, 𝐁_SI; 𝐫_χ, 𝐫_e; ĩ, f̃; ê_ν; b, b†; O_e, ε_e; R_ee, R̃_ee, S_ee; N_cell; q_ref, σ_ref; and ψ̃_{plm},
    ψ̃_{p_ejl′m′} (2.2 continuum waves, found by the checker, not listed in (h)). A.8 prints f_crys (was f_crystal). Still open, outside the

78. **Merge of the accepted Chapter 8 and App. D.24–D.28 (SEAMS 8.1–8.8 with their revision blocks of 2026-11-02, which contain the
    D.24–D.28 rows; 2026-11-02).** The new rows follow the planned Chap. 8 rows (after ϑ_k), in section order. Where a revision block and
    the first proposal differ, the revision block and the tex decide.
    (a) Renames applied in the tex at this merge: the pair propagator 𝖫, 𝖫⁰, 𝖫̄ of D.12 → 𝔏, 𝔏⁰, 𝔏̄ (all eleven lines of D-12.tex that
    print it, each the pair propagator in context, and the sentence "written sans-serif like 𝖦" → "written in fraktur"), so that 𝖫 is
    the likelihood of C.14 and 8.8 only (closes the 𝖫 item of 74(c) the other way round). s4-9.tex prints no symbol for the propagator
    (it names it in words), so it needed no edit. 𝒫^{eh} was not used: 𝒫 is the sector-projector family of 8.6 (𝒫_ad, 𝒫_0, 𝒫_+, 𝒫_BO,
    𝒫_A, 𝒫^qp_{n,m}) and sans-serif 𝖯 is the inversion of 𝖯𝖳 (8.6, 8.7, D.28); 𝔏 was free. Bare μ (reduced mass) in 8.2 (the Born
    amplitude \|f\| = μ\|Ṽ\|/2π) and D.24 (q·v = q²/2μ) → μ_χT (entry 31). Renames the Chapter 8 revision already made (the rows follow
    the text): C.20's bath spectral density 𝒥(ω) → J_s(ω) (C.20, E.22), which frees 𝒥 for the spin current 𝒥^{αβ}_s; 𝒬_{n,m}, 𝒬_A →
    𝒫^qp_{n,m}, 𝒫_A (8.6, 8.7, D.28; 𝒬 is the boosted momentum of 10.9, 𝒬(t) the C.20 function); 𝒜_f, 𝒜_ν → 𝒜^off_f, 𝒜^off_ν (8.6,
    D.28; 𝒜_fi is the transition amplitude of D.25); the carrier kinetic energies of 8.7, ε_e, ε_h → E_e, E_h (ε_e(q, ω) of A.7 and 8.4
    keeps the electron-channel dielectric function); the levels g, e of WE 8-sectors → \|lo⟩, \|up⟩; S⁰ of WE 8-pairs → S^IP (S⁰ is the
    zero-phonon sector).
    (b) Adopted from the SEAMs: v̄_χ, c_i^{(s)}, 𝐒_χ, \|𝐒_χ\|, 𝐒_e, 𝐒_N, 𝒥^{αβ}_s, 𝐉^nuc_I, J_nuc, ⟨S_p⟩, ⟨S_n⟩, Λ_F, m_ν̃, Tr_χ,

79. **Merge of the accepted Chapter 10 and App. D.29–D.30 (SEAMS 10.1–10.10 with their "Revision after the Chapter 10 review" blocks,
    which contain the D.29 and D.30 rows; ledgers/REVIEWS/ch10/editor-notes.md and revision.md; 2026-11-02).** D.31 and D.32 serve
    Chap. 11 and are not part of this merge. The new rows follow the planned Chap. 10 rows (after v_χ,max), in section order. Where a
    revision block and the first proposal differ, the revision block and the tex decide.
    (a) Renames. Made by the Chapter 10 revision (the rows follow the text): 𝒢_GS → 𝒢_rel (10.2; banned label; E.16 l.46–52 and F-rows
    l.76 still print \mathrm{GS}, App. E pass 2); \varOmega (pole of a self-coupled pair) → E_cp and χ⁰(0), χ(0), χ^TD → R⁰(0), R(0),
    R^TD(0) (10.5; Ω is the cell volume, χ the dark matter); bare Λ → Λ_NP (10.8) and → E_cut (Limit 10.7b caption; Λ is the final-state
    class of 8.7); K, K̄ → K_pair, K̄_pair (10.8; K is a zone point, K̄ the mean kinetic energy per electron); script 𝒯^{(2)}, 𝒯^C → italic
    T^{(2)}, T^C (10.8; T-matrix family of T_fi; 𝒯_e is the kinetic row); 𝒢_a → g^{φφ}_a (10.8; clashed with 𝒢_rel and 𝒢_VH); n_g → n_grp
    (10.9; n_g is the electron–phonon order of 𝔗); ⟨f\|T\|0⟩ → ⟨f\|p²/2m_e\|0⟩ in the Limit 10.7b caption (T is the temperature). Bare ξ
    (Bauer et al.'s normalization) and β_c are no longer printed: their proposed rows are withdrawn. Made at this merge: the Euler gamma
    function of 10.5 (Fermi factor, |\varGamma(2 + iξ_C)|²) → upright Γ(2 + iξ_C), following the Γ(z) row of C.20 (editor ruling on the
    flag of SEAM 10.5 and derivation review H6: \varGamma is a rate, upright Γ with an argument is the gamma function).
    (b) Adopted from the SEAMs: S̄⁰ (10.1, D.29); L̄, K_n, η_n^max, 𝐔_𝐑, ε_cl, Z^{(e)}_κ, Ω_{P,e} (10.2; the proposed S_ee^ac is printed
    as L_e^ac); Z_L, ℛ_pl, v_th, B⁺_pl, ε₂⁺ (10.3); j_L, j_Ti, 𝐣_T, 𝒥^{Ti,β}_e, 𝒥_T, ê_1, ê_2, v̄_L, 𝐯̄_T, r, r_med, δ^j_LF, S⁰_ab (10.4,
    D.30); ρ_λ0, ε₁^res, L^res, w_λ, C, E_cp, R^TD(0), H^exc_T (10.5); S⁰_core, ρ_c,κnlm, S^at_nl, N^acc_nl, δ_mv, δ_oo, H_oo, T_ij, E_ee,
    q_3d (10.6); Γ_T, Γ_L, 𝐉_a, φ₀, ε″, ε_∥, ε_⊥, f₁, f₂, 𝓃_at, R_𝒯𝒯, S_𝒯𝒯, 𝐅(ω), d_φee (10.7); G_S, G_P, G_V, G_A, G_F, Λ_NP, p̄, v̄,
    Ē, g^{φφ}_a, K_pair, K̄_pair, p_K, Γ_pair, g_eff, T^{(2)}_ab, T^C, ω_γ, 𝐤_γ, ê_γ, ω_C, σ_Th, H_sg (10.8); q_Δ, v_Δ, k_*, ω_line,
    q_line, E_χ, D_eff, σ_F, P_L, P_R, m_med, μ_M, Λ_d, Γ_vac (10.9); ℛ^𝔗, σ_ln ℛ (10.10). Added from the tex (no proposal): 𝐉_ind
    (10.7), γ_Lν, γ_Tν (Limit 10.7a), G_V,A, P_L,R, y_ee, y_χν̃ (10.8, 10.9), 𝐉_χT, 𝐣_eT, Γ_S, Γ_V (10.9), Π_T,L (Π_T, Π_L row), g^φ_𝒥

80. **Ch11 revision (2026-11-02, after the Ch11 reviews).** (a) Renames in Chap. 11 and App. D.31–D.32: O_A(q) (11.1, nucleon-number operator)

    𝔫_κ(p) (11.4, 11.5, D.32, nuclear momentum distribution) → 𝔣_κ(p), since 𝔫₁, 𝔫₂ are the interface densities of 20.3; ω̄ (11.2,
    weighted longitudinal frequency) → ω̄_pol, since ω̄ is 5.3's; q₀² = 2M_κω₀ (11.3, quoting Kahn et al.) → q_osc, since q₀ = αm_e;
    the cosine u of 11.7 → x (u stays a velocity); the depolarization factor N of 11.8 → N_dp; the 11.5 exponent ν in λ₃^ν → j (ν stays
    the branch index). (b) Kept with rows: c_κ is the per-species weight of a density coupling (11.3, used by 12.6 "of Sect. 11.3"), so
    12.6 needs no new symbol; c_k (integer k) are the cumulants of the self response (11.4, 11.5, D.32; c₃ also 12.5), against c_κ
    (Greek κ), c_ν (sound speed) and 𝔠_b (8.6) — a book-wide rename of the cumulants to a free letter is left to the editor, since 12.5
    (written in parallel) prints c₃. D (kick operator, 11.4) shadows D^an, D^dyn of 5.5 only with their superscripts. d (11.8, site
    difference) is local to 11.8; the nearest-neighbor distance of 11.5 is d_nn. (c) App. F rows (F-rows.tex l.282–286): done, they print ℋ_ℓ

**Editor confirmation (2026-11-01, after spine revision):** renames κ_mix → ζ, Φ_κκ′(t) → 𝒞_κκ′(t), N(0) → 𝒩_F, v_F (Dirac) → v_D, M(r) → M_enc(r), ⟨T⟩ → K̄ and clash entries 27–49 are confirmed.

**Editor decision (2026-11-01):** kinetic mixing ζ uses the sign convention of Lin, TASI lectures arXiv:1904.07915, Eq. (70): ℒ ⊃ +(ζ/2)F_μνF′^μν (corrected 2026-11-01: Lin's Eq. (70) has +κ/2, as used in C.9, Sect. 1.4 and A.9). Record in App. A.9 and B.2.
**Editor decisions (Chapter 1 drafting):** (i) c_ν (sound speed of branch ν) first used in Sect. 1.3, defined there qualitatively; precise in 5.2. (ii) v_min is written with an absolute value (covers exothermic dark matter); App. D.1's signed-form sentence is aligned at the Chapter 1 revision. (iii) Silicon lattice constant a = 5.431 Å cited to CODATA (NIST, lattice spacing of silicon); low-temperature indirect gap 1.17 eV cited to a public measurement at revision.
**Editor rulings (Appendix A):** the f-sum rule ∫₀^∞ ω L_e dω = (π/2)ω_p² holds at any temperature (it follows from the double commutator, which is T-independent), so A.7's statement stands; silicon's valence plasma energy is 16.6 eV (A.2) and Sect. 4.3 must use it.
**Editor rulings (Chapter 2 drafting):** v_C with upright C (a label, per STYLE) book-wide; App. A.2 and Sect. 2.3 are aligned at the Chapter 2 revision. κ, Z_κ, M_κ first appear in 2.3. a₀ = 1/(αm_e) (Bohr radius), as in 2.2.

**Editor decisions (App. C):** notation proposals in SEAMS/C.md accepted (upright K(s), E(s) with modulus s; Y^c_l; δ_Γ; μ, η in C.6; μ₀, μ₁ in C.8; script F). C.4 title "Singular denominators" accepted. A.3's Ω for a transition energy is renamed at the next Appendix A touch (Ω is the cell volume).
**Editor ruling (𝔗 anharmonic order, 2026-11-02):** n_A counts powers of the anharmonic expansion parameter λ ∼ ⟨u⟩/a (Van Hove ordering: Φ⁽³⁾ ∝ λ, Φ⁽⁴⁾ ∝ λ²) in the response, not vertex insertions. The three-phonon decay rate (two cubic vertices) and the first-order quartic shift are both n_A = 2; the leading anharmonic phonon self-energy is the n_A = 2 level. Sects. 5.7, 8.5 and App. E.8, E.11 use this.

83. **Volume 𝓥 (2026-11-03, notation fix).** The normalization (crystal) volume, printed until now as bare italic V, is the script
    𝓥 (macro `\Vol` = `\mathscr{V}`, boondox script of `mathalpha`, as `\nd`) in all chapters and appendices, in the E.2 Δ gauge
    (𝓥, 𝓥₀, E_1(𝓥), E_2(𝓥)), in the C.18 bulk modulus B = −𝓥 d P/d𝓥 and in the axis label of Fig. 4.1. Italic V is now a potential
    only (V(x), Ṽ(q), Ṽ_a, V_KS, V_eN, V^(3), the Dirac potential of C.15, the Born–Oppenheimer surface of 11.4–11.5, 12.5–12.6,
    D.16, D.32, D.34), a matrix element (|V|² of the 10.9 literature row), a coupling (V_k of 4.7), or a label (the mediator in m_V,
    group V, Ti–6Al–4V). Clash: the variance terms 𝒱_<, 𝒱_>, 𝒱_<> of 18.2/E.24 are calligraphic (`\mathcal{V}`, 𝒱 in this table),
    the volume is script (`\mathscr{V}`, 𝓥 in this table, as ledgers/check_notation.py spells \mathscr capitals); the two never meet
    in one equation. Not changed: Ω (cell volume), V_BZ (zone volume, E.13), and V_cell of the literature (D.29, App. B.2).

**Editor ruling (App E):** measured Si ε∞ = 11.7 throughout the book: the room-temperature value, cited to Franta et al. 2017 (11.69 at 300 K). Grüneisen parameters are written γ^G_{Qν} (superscript upright G) to avoid the clash with the linewidth γ_{Qν}.

**Editor ruling (App. E structure, user 2026-11-02):** there is no separate Appendix E′. Its fourteen computational-specification sections are App. E.14–E.27, labels app:E.14 … app:E.27, after the methods sections E.1–E.13.

**Editor ruling (Heisenberg convention, App. E review):** H = Σ_{⟨ij⟩} J^ex_ij S_i·S_j, with each pair counted once and J^ex > 0 antiferromagnetic. Then the ferromagnetic one-magnon dispersion is ω_Q = |S|[J^ex(Q) − J^ex(0)] (with J(Q) = Σ_j J_0j e^{iQ·R_j}, sign as appropriate). This is binding for 7.1, WE 7.2, 14.1 and D.19/D.38.

**Editor ruling (phonon self-energy, D.17):** Π_ν is in eV², and 𝖣_ν = 2ω_Qν/(ω² − ω²_Qν − Π_ν). The phonon linewidth γ^ep_Qν = −Im Π_ν/ω_Qν is a full width (FWHM). The λ_ep–linewidth relation uses 𝒩_F per spin. Binding for 6.4, 15.x and the E.9 cross-references. Nambu matrices are 𝗌₁ and 𝗌₂ with η; Holstein–Primakoff bosons are d_A and d_B; Euler's constant is γ_E. All accepted.

**Editor ruling (strain tensor, clash 76(c)):** the strain tensor is u_{αβ} book-wide (Landau convention, as in C.18). S_αβ (6.2) and ε^αβ (5.7) are to be renamed at the next notation pass, because ε is the dielectric function and S is spin or the structure factor.

**Editor ruling (𝔗 counting, 8.5):** every order in 𝔗 is the power of its expansion parameter in the LABELLED QUANTITY (the rate or response), not in the amplitude. This generalizes the n_A ruling. A one-vertex amplitude squared is therefore order 2; e.g. Born scattering is n_B = 2 in the rate. The planned F rows L7c, CW2-L1 and L17′ (n_B), LB1-9 and L-H3 (n_D) and L7d (n_g) are relabelled to rate order when written (see partII notes). L-Fano (n_B = 1, n_g = 1) is an interference cross term and stays as it is. x_R uses the arithmetic mean ⟨ω⟩ over the per-atom phonon DOS (Si 40.9 meV). 𝒦 labels the phonon content of the final state.

**Editor ruling R9 (Ch14 review, 2026-11-06; applied at the Ch14 revision):** Ω is the unit-cell volume everywhere; the two-sublattice formulas of 14.1, 14.2 and D.38 use Ω_ion (volume per magnetic ion). 14.5's ω_M, η_M → ω_soft, γ_soft (14.3 keeps ω_M = 2μ_Bμ₀M_s). The spectroscopic g of an ion is g_ion (bare g stays the vertex); the point-group element of 14.4 is 𝗁. Bare Δ in 14.2: the gap is ω_gap, the detuning δ_mp. The target linewidth of 14.6 is Γ_tg (Γ_T is the dark-photon rate of 10.7; E.26 still prints Γ_T, owed at the next App. E pass). λ_S is the spin fraction of the ionic angular momentum (row corrected). The dispersion mass of the YIG uniform mode is M_sw.

100. **App. B, C, E post-revision fixes (2026-11-07, after ledgers/REVIEWS/appCEB-postrev-check.md).** (a) B.1 (figscripts/appB/gen_B1.py, rerun; B-1.tex not hand-edited): e^pz_αβγ and e^pz_zzz print as e^{\mathrm{pz}} (they printed as the exponential), y_χν̃ as y_{\chi\tilde\nu}, E_<, E_>, 𝒱_<> with the < or > as the script, ρ_t∗ρ_t∗ρ_t̄ with \ast; value lists such as (n = 0, 1, 2) stay in one math group; "stream a" sets a as math; v_C + v_CRv_C, 𝐮·J_z𝐩, exp(α_dd† − α_d*d), ρ_χσ̄_eη/m_χ and the 16.4 symbols with 𝔬, 𝔵 in a subscript (A^λ_𝔬𝔵, F_𝔬𝔵, f^(𝔬)_0𝔵, φ_co′, φ_co′′′, φ_vo′′, ρ_0x′^o) split at the right letter. Rows corrected: row e^pz_zzz (11.8) gets its meaning, dominant piezoelectric element, and unit; σ_ℛ leaves the 11.7 row (it was the checker's reading of σ_ln ℛ, which has its own row of 10.10; the checker now accepts σ_ℛ everywhere); the "as printed" remarks leave the units of 𝒮_φ, 𝒮_sig, 𝒮_n (now eV⁻²; (readout unit)² × time) and of v_C^2D, v_C^1D, v_C^L, and the meaning of E_r, q̃; the bare 𝔫 leaves the meaning of the 20.3 transmission row; the parenthesis of row ξ_a about E.27's ξ_i is deleted (obsolete since entry 99(a)). (b) New rows for the 104 gaps of check_notation.py --files 'appB/*.tex' 'appC/*.tex' 'appE/*.tex' (B 26, C 30, E 48), symbols spelled as in the tex: F̃_μν, F̃² (C.9); w_ξ, F_model (C.10); m̄; Ȳ(x); S_n, S_{N(E)} (C.11); ℬ_h, Φ_h, 𝛌_h, 𝗀_{λ_h}, 𝐰_h (C.12); I_a, ℓ_a, u_a (C.13); z_CL (C.14); H_rad, dΩ_k̂, 𝐉̃(𝐤, ω) (C.16); H_L, H_R; H_FK (C.20); ω_LO (E.14); S_λ(𝐪, ω) (E.15); v_χ (E.16); t_max, S_coh^(2)(𝐪, ω) (E.17); N_core, 𝐩_red, 𝔯_W(𝐪, ω_e) (E.18); P_wall(𝐧̂); L_th, 𝖬_λλ′, b_λ, 𝒩^ch_λ; P_K(𝐮), E_K, H_CF(𝐮) (E.19); R_nℓ(r), j_K, ⟨j_K(q)⟩; a_D, v_D,x, v_D,y, v_D,z, ε_bg,zz; N_src, N_corr; T_N, T_C (E.20); 𝐤_ι; R_OPW(E_kin) (E.21); Ψ_L, Ψ_R; ε_hop (E.22); 𝓃_pair, C_Aug, r_tr (E.23); Γ_ph,loss, w_mem; 𝒢_Λ(z), x_e, x_h (E.24); N_meas; s_ℓ(E_ath), D_X, S_surf, L_X, s_hard, s_soft; f_coll^(p) (E.25). The row X_αβ, f_me (14.2) and its rendering in App. B.1 still print X_αβ: the row is to read u_αβ, f_me with B₁Σ_αm_α²u_αα + B₂Σ_{α≠β}m_αm_βu_αβ, and the rename note is obsolete (append-only here; for the App. B owner). check_notation.py --files 'ch14/*.tex': 0 gaps. 101. **Light pass, part A (2026-11-07; brief lightpass-A).** (a) 12.3 and 12.6 now print the per-nucleon cross-section of a coupling equal on protons and neutrons as σ_p, the σ_p of 1.4, 11.1 and 17.1 (entry 88(a) and the σ_p (17.1, 17.3, 17.5) row); σ_n is again only the Drude conductivity of the normal film (15.4). The reduced mass in those formulas stays μ_χn (row μ_χn, with the nucleon mass of the scripts). (b) No new symbol. WE 7.3 now uses the measured-structure row of ZrTe₅ (Δ_D = 11.8 meV, the value of 9.2, 9.3 and 16.1); the relaxed-cell Δ_D = 15.6 meV is named there once as the other computed row, so the Δ_D row is unchanged.

103. **Light pass, part C (2026-11-07; editor rulings after lightpass-A/B).** (a) Rename: C.9's field strength of the shifted photon, F̃_μν = ∂_μÃ_ν − ∂_νÃ_μ, and its square F̃² are now F̄_μν (`\bar F_{\mu\nu}`) and F̄², defined in place in C.9 (eqs. C.9-mix → C.9-mixed); F̃^μν is again only the dual field strength ½ε^μνρσF_ρσ of 1.4 (row A_μ, F_μν, Ã_μ; F̃^μν). The photon after the redefinition keeps the name Ã_μ. F′_μν stays the dark-photon field strength, which is why the prime was not used. The row F̃_μν, F̃² (C.9) is to read "F̄_μν, F̄² | field strength ∂_μÃ_ν − ∂_νÃ_μ of the photon after the mixing redefinition; its square F̄_μνF̄^μν (C.9) | eV²; eV⁴ | C.9" (append-only here; check_notation reports F̄, F̄_μν, F̄^μν in C-9 as gaps until the row is edited, and App. B.1 follows at the gen_B1.py rerun). This settles the first clash of entry 100(d). (b) Scope notes, no rename (the meanings never share a section, and the App. E files are not touched in this pass): ω_LO (E.14, and the literature cutoff Θ(ω_LO − ω) of Campbell-Deem et al. quoted in App. F) is the ω_L of 5.5/6.2 for the single LO branch of GaAs, written with the branch label of the Fröhlich literature; one quantity, two spellings, never in one passage. E.24's italic x_e, x_h (seed energies above the band edges, eV) appear only in E.24; the bold positions 𝐱_χ, 𝐱_e appear only in 2.1, 2.2 and C.9; weight and units separate them. E.23's λ_tr (electron–hole pairs per unit length of a Gaussian track, with r_tr) is local to E.23 and needs a row next to 𝓃_pair, C_Aug, r_tr; the transport coupling λ_tr of 15.4 (also E.20, F-rows, the Ch15 summary) never appears in E.23; if the App. E ledger merge prefers a rename, the track density becomes N′_tr or 𝓃_lin, a change confined to E-23.tex (four displays and one sentence). (c) No other symbol changes: 6.4 now states that its elastic constants are the room-temperature set of its source and gives the zero-temperature sensitivity (λ_ep 0.415, ω_log 259 K, two-peak 0.385); 18.5 quotes the germanium exciton binding as a few meV, 2.5 and 5 meV in absorption (row E_x^b unchanged); 9.2, 17.1, 17.5 and 17.6 carry the Ge numbers of a = 5.658 Å (c_LA,max 5.60 km/s, c_NR1 5.321 km/s), and 17.6, 21.7 and 22.2 the database offset 4.9 % that follows from them.
